- **space**: 你是一个航天工程专家，精通轨道力学、火箭推进、卫星系统、CFD模拟和NASA/ESA标准，并且精通各种卫星遥感知识，卫星影像知识等。你的目标是注入航天领域知识到产品/技术方案中。 ([zesion21/dsh-project-template](https://github.com/zesion21/dsh-project-template/tree/HEAD/.dsh/skills/space) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **space**: The Mac's disk space, through Colin's ~/space pathways and the deck's Space tile. Use for "what's eating my disk", "how much space do I have", "clean up", "free up space", "show the Space tile", "/deck:space", or any question about disk usage, caches, node_modules, Downloads or iCloud archiving. The CLI is `node "$DECK_SPACE" open` (show the pane) and `node "$DECK_SPACE" status|list|rules|scan` (read-only, passed to ~/space/bin/space.mjs). Nothing is deleted or moved by you. Colin decides in the tile. ([cgrilson7/deck](https://github.com/cgrilson7/deck/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/space))
- **space**: จัดการ space (= project หรือหัวข้อ) ใน Tamra knowledge base — สร้าง space ใหม่ได้ทุก credential ที่มี kb:write (kb_space_create); ดูทุก space, แก้ policy/ชื่อ, เพิ่มหรือถอดสมาชิกต้องใช้ kb_admin — เรียกด้วย /tamra:space list | /tamra:space &lt;code&gt; [PREFIX] [ชื่อ] [--policy none|self] [--pack software-dev|core] [--sensitive] | /tamra:space &lt;code&gt; set &lt;field&gt;=&lt;value&gt; | /tamra:space &lt;code&gt; add|remove &lt;principal&gt; [role] ([mounchons/mflow-marketplace](https://github.com/mounchons/mflow-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/tamra/skills/space))
- **space**: Layout, spacing and rhythm.
